### Identity (reviewed)

Original native arcade game in which Mr Aldo catches ceiling rain; source implements waves, character animation, hazards, score, music and fast display primitives.

### Identity (reviewed)

T. Zoerner’s own three early VIC-20 games recovered by retyping paper hex dumps, disassembling with dxa, reconstructing labels/comments and repairing code. Keep as one coherent author-restoration collection.

### Identity (reviewed)

Robert Hurst reconstructed his hand-coded 1984 maze game from recovered cassette data and a VICMON disassembly, aiming for exact reassembly of the original.
- https://github.com/theflyingape/quikman/blob/e969b17e0230506216b9ef59cd756382660e7b7d/quikman.s
- https://github.com/theflyingape/quikman/blob/e969b17e0230506216b9ef59cd756382660e7b7d/quikman.bat

- https://github.com/theflyingape/quikman/tree/e969b17e0230506216b9ef59cd756382660e7b7d

### Classification (reviewed)

1,637-line commented source covers monster movement, scoring, sprite masking/background restoration and sound. Author reports matching original binary after comparison; not independently verified. Artistic License 2.0 in LICENSE.

Annotated disassembly of David Snider’s Serpentine, VIC-20 port by Creative Software, documenting its bitmap-style character rendering and snake/egg/frog systems.

### Identity (reviewed)

Eleven VIC-20 cartridge games reconstructed with da65 and relabelled for ca65, including relocatable absolute references and split-bank treatment of 16KB titles. Keep as one repository-level disassembly collection.
- https://github.com/dhansel/vic20games/blob/532cd4a4c269a8dca981678b0a1ffeb8c7b5222d/pacman.asm

### Identity (reviewed)

Dave Curran’s 2026 6502 rewrite of Doug Smoak’s July 1983 Compute! BASIC type-in game. Original BASIC listing and magazine scans accompany the implementation.

### Identity (reviewed)

Malcolm Tyrrell’s original 2011–2012 four-kilobyte real-time puzzle game for unexpanded VIC-20. One-button red/green mood switching controls whether the moving player responds to floor symbols, keys and switches.
- https://github.com/Malcohol/Fickle-Vic20/blob/7b65879aac37c542fd42ae0335c80834f8748aac/src/Source.s
- https://github.com/Malcohol/Fickle-Vic20/blob/7b65879aac37c542fd42ae0335c80834f8748aac/src/Common.s

- https://github.com/Malcohol/Fickle-Vic20/commit/7b65879aac37c542fd42ae0335c80834f8748aac

### Classification (reviewed)

4,428-line main assembly with explicit VIC registers/IRQ/NMI, PAL/NTSC handling, game state, symbols, sprites and level logic. Linker layouts and decompression integration supplied. Main source AGPL-3.0. Forked Exomizer has its own free-software licence; README states level copyright and asks permission for non-free reuse.

### Identity (reviewed)

New small-footprint TCP/IP and MacIP stack linked into native Macintosh applications, plus a MacTCP-compatible driver, for a 512Ke with System 6 over LocalTalk.

### Identity (reviewed)

Substantial derivative of Marcio Teixeira MiniVNC adding native PowerPC CFM builds, Retro68 integration, background operation and true-color wire-format fixes, while retaining classic 68k builds.
- https://github.com/felipedbene/mac-minivnc/blob/e04fd2b9754ad15796f1248bb3c927f65f6e9463/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Restored 1997-2000 Turbo Pascal-era PC utility that reads and writes physical Archimedes floppy sectors into emulator-compatible disk images, including later F-format improvements.
- https://github.com/picosonic/arcimage/blob/a91adb699cc5f4bad5d5663c57436f7ddb5bc092/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual sector-read/write and disk-parameter implementation present. Base README warns formatting is broken; v1.1.2 separately claims F-format formatting/improved errors. MIT release by Jasper Renow-Clarke (2020); original author chronology retained.

A substantial TypeScript/browser port of the released Macintosh Continuum source, translating game physics, shots, collision and rendering into modules rather than executing the original game binary.

Actual moveShot translation cites original Play.c line range. ### Identity (reviewed)

Riley Lannon’s NES assembly project, inspired/guided by the NESdev Galaxy Patrol example. Main source implements a scrolling asteroid/fuel game, game states, player movement and collision handling; an included sound engine remains partial. This is a distinct educational authored version, not a commercial disassembly.

Authored NES Snake game with a modular NESASM implementation. Game code covers persistent direction, movement ticks, pause/game-over states, snake growth and rendering, with CHR, palettes and nametables committed alongside it.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable C++ Game Boy emulator targeting Nintendo 3DS. Pinned implementation contains SM83 operand/opcode execution, PPU scanline rendering, cartridge memory banking, APU, timer, joypad, ROM browsing and a platform/core separation. It loads general Game Boy ROMs rather than wrapping one game.
- https://github.com/BasHeemskerk/GBEMU/blob/c82e23d45fc74faec3a1209232f25724a25283fc/source/gb/cpu.cpp
- https://github.com/BasHeemskerk/GBEMU/blob/c82e23d45fc74faec3a1209232f25724a25283fc/source/gb/ppu.cpp

Work in progress; README says games playable but SRAM saving absent. DS/PSP/PC are planned, not supported output targets. Root MIT LICENSE (2025), plus older MIT LICENSE.txt crediting Brandon W. 2017; retain both notices and investigate component provenance before redistribution.

Robert Louie’s reusable TypeScript Game Boy emulator with independent CPU operation families, GPU/APU/timer/memory/cartridge subsystems and browser UI/examples. Gameboy.runFrame coordinates arbitrary cartridge execution and frame rendering rather than embedding one game.

### Identity (reviewed)

Lucas Falcão and Flavio Freitas’s SGDK class-project source for a medieval two-player projectile-parrying game. Actual code contains arena, knight/wizard sprites, directional controls, attack handler and display/physics routines. Distinct from the tracked 1986 Apple II game sharing the title.

Prototype only: main labels sprite work under construction, draws debug positions and has unfinished projectile/parrying design. Promote only as source-project prototype, not finished game. Root LICENSE is LGPL version 3; bundled emulator binaries/third-party assets may carry independent terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native SGDK/C work-in-progress isometric racing/adventure source by realbrucest. Main loop initializes scenario, car/camera and scrolling; car.c implements rotation-derived velocity, sprite direction frames and motion, alongside scenario, pickup, weapon, enemy, HUD and screen modules.

### Identity (reviewed)

Authored C/SGDK demake of Paper.io 1 for Genesis, with tile-map territory/path data, human/computer players, movement, claim/fill calculations, score and rendering implemented in src/main.c. Source-level demake rather than game-specific CPU emulation.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable Windows 9x VxD traps OPL/AdLib and MPU-401 I/O from DOS sessions and streams captured writes to user-mode software audio rendering. Own driver/glue implementation with several bundled synthesis backends.
- https://github.com/sdz-mods/VOPL3/blob/9bb3634d05ab49a5f0fd1e7f9613af99b4be7ead/README.md

- https://github.com/sdz-mods/VOPL3/commit/9bb3634d05ab49a5f0fd1e7f9613af99b4be7ead

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed ring-0 I/O trapping and user-mode waveOut/MIDI path. General compatibility subsystem, not a game-specific wrapper. Own code MIT; Nuked backends LGPL-2.1, DBOPL GPL-2.0-or-later, ymfm BSD-3-Clause. DBOPL renderer has GPL whole-program requirements; Microsoft SBEMUL.SYS is not distributed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preservation repository for the historical JUDAS 2.10d DOS sound library: module/sample playback, software mixers and direct Sound Blaster, GUS, AC97 and HDA hardware support.
- https://github.com/volkertb/JUDAS/blob/8f25e0e9ac11e4b1b9679b8652ac257a50c5da2e/README.TXT

### Classification (reviewed)

Real hardware initialization and module playback source present. Repository is a historical code preservation effort, not a separate invented modern implementation. GPL release intent documented, but LICENSE.md explicitly discusses ambiguity in exact version and HDA-only versus whole-project wording. Do not present licensing as unambiguously GPL-2.0-only.

### Identity (reviewed)

Complete source/assets of Max and Morgan McGuire's original Windows artillery game, housed in the xenon directory of Morgan's multi-project archive; inspired by Scorched Earth, not the unrelated Bitmap Brothers Xenon shooter.

### Identity (reviewed)

Gardens Point Modula-2 compiler frontend with a CIL emitter, preserving the historical compiler lineage while enabling unmanaged Modula-2 applications on .NET. Source includes semantic traversal, parser, CIL writer and C# runtime modules.

### Build (reviewed)

mit/kldcp/kldcp.20 selects device modules and .INSRTs PRM11, BEG11, CONS11, disk/floppy/tape services before .END $ILOAD. Converted to PALX from DEC Rev 7 per source history. No assembly verified. Build flags remain unknown; no candidate software executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Runs on PDP-11 KL10 frontend to control PDP-10 memory/microcode/diagnostics through DTE20. The KL10 is the controlled machine, not this console program’s CPU.

## Research event notes

Bounded broad discovery with programmatic URL screening before pinned implementation/build/licence inspection and title/lineage deduplication. Not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
